Rule 3338:
If level of evidence LevelOfEvidence C AND source grade record ?a, then type SourceGradeRecord.

Formal Rule:
Head: ?a  type  SourceGradeRecord
Body: ?c  hasLevelOfEvidence  LevelOfEvidence_C  ?c  hasSourceGradeRecord  ?a
